```python
 
    
def
 
encrypt_response_with_aes(response_dict,
 
aes_key):
 
        
response_iv
 
=
 
os.urandom(16)
 
        
cipher_encrypt
 
=
 
Cipher(algorithms.AES(aes_key),
 
modes.CBC(response_iv),
 
backend=default_backend())
 
        
encryptor
 
=
 
cipher_encrypt.encryptor()
 
        
json_data_bytes
 
=
 
json.dumps(response_dict).encode("utf-8")
 
        
padded_data
 
=
 
pkcs7_pad(json_data_bytes)
 
        
encrypted_data
 
=
 
encryptor .update(padded_data)
 
+
 
encryptor .finalize()
 
        
return
 
{
 
            
"iv":
 
base64.b64encode(response_iv).decode("utf-8"),
 
            
"data":
 
base64.b64encode(encrypted_data).decode("utf-8")
 
        
}
```

def
 
populate_users_table(self):

def
 
add_user(self):
 
        
if
 
self.app.server_error_active:
 
return
 
        
username
 
=
 
self.username_entry .get().strip()
 
        
email
 
=
 
self.email_entry .get().strip()
 
        
password
 
=
 
generate_strong_password()
 
#
 
Generate
 
password
 
client-side
 
        
id_number
 
=
 
self.id_entry .get().strip()
 
        
is_admin_val
 
=
 
1
 
if
 
self.is_admin_var .get()
 
else
 
0
 
 
        
if
 
not
 
username:
 
            
messagebox.showerror("Error",
 
"Username
 
is
 
required.",
 
parent=self.root)
 
            
return
 
        
if
 
not
 
is_valid_email(email):
 
            
messagebox.showerror("Invalid
 
Email",
 
"Please
 
enter
 
a
 
valid
 
email
 
address.",
 
parent=self.root)
 
            
return
 
        
if
 
not
 
id_number .isdigit()
 
or
 
len(id_number)
 
!=
 
9:
 
            
messagebox.showerror("Invalid
 
ID",
 
"ID
 
number
 
must
 
be
 
9
 
digits.",
 
parent=self.root)
 
            
return
 
 
        
response
 
=
 
self.app.safe_network_request({
 
            
"type":
 
"add_user",
 
            
"username":
 
username,
 
            
"email":
 
email,
 
            
"password":
 
password,
 
#
 
Server
 
will
 
hash
 
this
 
password
 
for
 
storage
 
            
"raw_password":
 
password,
 
#
 
For
 
sending
 
in
 
email
 
152
 
עילי
 
נגלר
 
IlayDF
 
            
"id_number":
 
id_number ,
 
            
"is_admin":
 
is_admin_val
 
#
 
Send
 
the
 
admin
 
status
 
        
},
 
"AdminDashboard.add_user")
 
 
        
if
 
response
 
is
 
None:
 
return
 
#
 
Server
 
error
 
handled
 
 
        
if
 
response.get("status")
 
==
 
"ok":
 
            
messagebox.showinfo("Success",
 
"User
 
added
 
successfully .
 
Password
 
sent
 
to
 
email.",
 
parent=self.root)
 
            
self.populate_users_table()
 
#
 
Refresh
 
the
 
list
 
            
#
 
Clear
 
entry
 
fields
 
            
self.username_entry .delete(0,
 
"end")
 
            
self.email_entry .delete(0,
 
"end")
 
            
self.id_entry .delete(0,
 
"end")
 
            
self.is_admin_var .set(False)
 
        
else:
 
            
messagebox.showerror("Error",
 
response.get("message",
 
"Failed
 
to
 
add
 
user."),
 
parent=self.root)

show_manage_user_data(self):

self.search_var

def
 
load_users_to_selector(self):
 
#
 
Also
 
called
 
by
 
update_user_selector
 
        
if
 
self.app.server_error_active:
 
return
 
        
current_search
 
=
 
self.search_var .get().lower()
 
if
 
hasattr(self,
 
'search_var')
 
else
 
""
 
        
response
 
=
 
self.app.safe_network_request({"type":
 
"get_all_users"},
 
"AdminDashboard.load_users_to_selector")
 
        
if
 
response
 
is
 
None:
 
self.user_selector .configure(values=[]);
 
return
 
 
        
if
 
response.get("status")
 
==
 
"ok":
 
            
users
 
=
 
response.get("data",
 
[])
 
            
#
 
Filter
 
out
 
admins
 
AND
 
filter
 
by
 
search
 
term
 
            
regular_usernames
 
=
 
[u[1]
 
for
 
u
 
in
 
users
 
if
 
not
 
u[2]
 
and
 
(not
 
current_search
 
or
 
current_search
 
in
 
u[1].lower())]
 
            
self.user_selector .configure(values=regular_usernames)
 
            
if
 
not
 
regular_usernames
 
and
 
current_search:
 
#
 
if
 
search
 
yielded
 
no
 
results
 
                 
self.user_selector .set("No
 
matching
 
users")
 
            
elif
 
not
 
regular_usernames:
 
                 
self.user_selector .set("No
 
users
 
available")
 
            
elif
 
self.selected_user_var .get()
 
not
 
in
 
regular_usernames:
 
#
 
If
 
current
 
selection
 
invalid
 
or
 
empty
 
                
if
 
regular_usernames
 
:
 
self.selected_user_var .set(regular_usernames[0])
 
#
 
Select
 
first
 
if
 
list
 
not
 
empty
 
                
else:
 
self.selected_user_var .set("")
 
#
 
Clear
 
if
 
list
 
became
 
empty
 
        
else:
 
            
self.user_selector .configure(values=[])
 
            
self.user_selector .set("Failed
 
to
 
load
 
users")
 
        
#
 
After
 
updating
 
values,
 
if
 
a
 
user
 
is
 
selected,
 
trigger
 
data
 
load.
 
        
#
 
This
 
is
 
now
 
handled
 
by
 
combobox
 
command
 
or
 
explicit
 
call
 
after
 
setting
 
selection.
 
        
if
 
self.selected_user_var .get()
 
and
 
self.selected_user_var .get()
 
not
 
in
 
["No
 
matching
 
users",
 
"Failed
 
to
 
load
 
users",
 
"No
 
users
 
available"]:
 
            
self.load_user_data()
 
#
 
Load
 
data
 
for
 
the
 
(newly)
 
selected
 
user
 
        
else:
 
            
self.clear_user_data_fields()
 
#
 
Clear
 
fields
 
if
 
no
 
valid
 
user
 
selected

def
 
generate_strong_password(length=10):
 
    
#
 
Ensure
 
there's
 
at
 
least
 
one
 
of
 
each
 
required
 
type
 
if
 
stricter
 
rules
 
apply
 
170
 
עילי
 
נגלר
 
IlayDF
 
    
#
 
For
 
now,
 
simple
 
random
 
choice
 
from
 
a
 
combined
 
set
 
    
chars
 
=
 
string.ascii_letters
 
+
 
string.digits
 
+
 
"!@#$%^&*()"
 
    
#
 
Make
 
sure
 
length
 
is
 
reasonable
 
    
length
 
=
 
max(8,
 
min(length,
 
16))
 
#
 
e.g.
 
enforce
 
8-16
 
char
 
passwords
 
    
return
 
''.join(random.choices(chars,
 
k=length))

safe_entry_delete(entry):

except

print(f"[safe_entry_delete]

{e}")

def
 
pkcs7_pad(data,
 
block_size=16):

=
 
block_size
 
-
 
len(data)
 
%
 
block_size
 
171

נגלר

def
 
pkcs7_unpad(data):
 
    
if
 
not
 
data:
 
        
raise
 
ValueError("Cannot
 
unpad
 
empty
 
data")
 
    
pad_len
 
=
 
data[-1]
 
    
if
 
pad_len
 
>
 
len(data)
 
or
 
pad_len
 
==
 
0:
 
        
raise
 
ValueError("Invalid
 
PKCS#7
 
padding
 
or
 
RCE
 
attempt")
 
    
for
 
i
 
in
 
range(1,
 
pad_len
 
+
 
1):
 
        
if
 
data[-i]
 
!=
 
pad_len:
 
            
raise
 
ValueError("Invalid
 
PKCS#7
 
padding
 
bytes
 
or
 
RCE
 
attempt")
 
    
return
 
data[:-pad_len]

class
 
NetworkClient:

__init__(self):

def
 
pkcs7_pad(data,
 
block_size=16):
 
    
pad_len
 
=
 
block_size
 
-
 
len(data)
 
%
 
block_size
 
    
return
 
data
 
+
 
bytes([pad_len]
 
*
 
pad_len)
 
 
def
 
pkcs7_unpad(data):
 
    
if
 
not
 
data:
 
raise
 
ValueError("Cannot
 
unpad
 
empty
 
data")
 
    
pad_len
 
=
 
data[-1]
 
    
if
 
pad_len
 
>
 
len(data)
 
or
 
pad_len
 
==
 
0:
 
raise
 
ValueError("Invalid
 
PKCS#7
 
padding
 
length")
 
    
for
 
i
 
in
 
range(1,
 
pad_len
 
+
 
1):
 
        
if
 
data[-i]
 
!=
 
pad_len:
 
raise
 
ValueError("Invalid
 
PKCS#7
 
padding
 
bytes")
 
    
return
 
data[:-pad_len]

def
 
handle_request(request):
 
    
rtype
 
=
 
request.get("type")
 
    
client_ip
 
=
 
request.get('request_ip',
 
'Unknown
 
IP')
 
#
 
Get
 
IP
 
safely
 
for
 
logging
 
 
    
#
 
Basic
 
logging
 
for
 
all
 
request
 
types
 
    
#
 
print(f"Handling
 
request
 
of
 
type:
 
{rtype}
 
from
 
{client_ip}")
 
 
    
if
 
rtype
 
==
 
"get_public_key":
 
        
pem
 
=
 
PUBLIC_KEY .public_bytes(
 
            
encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
 
            
format=serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo
 
        
).decode()
 
        
return
 
{"status":
 
"ok",
 
"key":
 
pem}

...
 
(All
 
other
 
specific
 
rtype
 
handlers
 
remain
 
unchanged
 
from
 
the
 
previous
 
full

elif

==

email
 
=
 
check_user_credentials(username,
 
password)
 
        
if
 
success:
 
            
conn_db
 
=
 
connect_db()
 
            
cursor
 
=
 
conn_db.cursor()
 
            
cursor .execute("SELECT
 
phone,
 
address,
 
age
 
FROM
 
usersDB
 
WHERE
 
id=?",
 
(user_id,))
 
            
user_row
 
=
 
cursor .fetchone()
 
            
conn_db.close()
 
            
first_login
 
=
 
not
 
user_row
 
or
 
any(val
 
is
 
None
 
or
 
str(val).strip()
 
==
 
""
 
for
 
val
 
in
 
user_row)
 
            
return
 
{"status":
 
"ok",
 
"message":
 
message,
 
"is_admin":
 
is_admin,
 
"user_id":
 
user_id,
 
"email":
 
email,
 
"first_login":
 
first_login}
 
        
else:
 
            
return
 
{"status":
 
"error",
 
"message":
 
message}

def
 
encrypt_response_with_aes(response_dict,
 
aes_key):
 
    
response_iv
 
=
 
os.urandom(16)
 
    
cipher_encrypt
 
=
 
Cipher(algorithms.AES(aes_key),
 
modes.CBC(response_iv),
 
backend=default_backend())
 
    
encryptor
 
=
 
cipher_encrypt.encryptor()
 
    
json_data_bytes
 
=
 
json.dumps(response_dict).encode('utf-8')
 
    
padded_data
 
=
 
pkcs7_pad(json_data_bytes)
 
    
encrypted_data
 
=
 
encryptor .update(padded_data)
 
+
 
encryptor .finalize()
 
    
return
 
{
 
        
"iv":
 
base64.b64encode(response_iv).decode('utf-8'),
 
        
"data":
 
base64.b64encode(encrypted_data).decode('utf-8')
 
    
}

def
 
start_server():
 
    
server
 
=
 
socket.socket(socket.AF_INET ,
 
socket.SOCK_STREAM)
 
    
server .setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET ,
 
socket.SO_REUSEADDR,
 
1)
 
    
try:
 
        
server .bind((SER VER_IP ,
 
SERVER_POR T))
 
    
except
 
OSError
 
as
 
e:
 
        
print(f"!!!
 
SERVER
 
BIND
 
FAILED:
 
{e}
 
!!!")
 
        
print("Check
 
if
 
another
 
process
 
is
 
using
 
the
 
port,
 
or
 
if
 
IP
 
is
 
correct.")
 
        
return
 
 
    
server .listen()
 
    
print(f"[LISTENING]
 
Server
 
running
 
on
 
{SER VER_IP}:{SER VER_POR T}")
 
    
try:
 
        
while
 
True:
 
            
try:
 
                
conn,
 
addr
 
=
 
server .accept()
 
                
executor .submit(handle_client,
 
conn,
 
addr)
 
            
except
 
OSError
 
as
 
e:
 
                
print(f"Server
 
accept
 
error:
 
{e}.
 
Shutting
 
down
 
executor .")
 
                
break
 
    
except
 
KeyboardInterrupt:
 
        
print("\n[SHUTTING
 
DOWN]
 
Keyboard
 
interrupt
 
received.
 
Server
 
is
 
shutting
 
down.")
 
    
except
 
Exception
 
as
 
e_main_loop:
 
        
print(f"[CRITICAL
 
SERVER
 
ERROR]
 
Main
 
loop
 
error:
 
{e_main_loop}")
 
    
finally:
 
        
print("Closing
 
server
 
socket
 
and
 
shutting
 
down
 
thread
 
pool...")
 
        
executor .shutdown(wait=T rue)
 
        
server .close()
 
        
print("[CLOSED]
 
Server
 
has
 
closed
 
gracefully .")

def
 
hash_password(password):
 
    
return
 
hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()
 
 
 
def
 
hash_id_number(id_number):
 
    
return
 
hashlib.sha256(id_number .encode()).hexdigest()

is_strong_password(password):
